# ValuePickr Open Screen — Running Analysis

This is a living document for the NEW, separate open-screen project (all active ValuePickr
company threads, not the original 108-stock curated list — see `docs/00_SCREEN_RANKING.docx`
here, which is distinct from `docs/00_FINAL_RANKING.docx` in the original project). Append
only, organized per-stock, dated entries. Never delete or rewrite past entries.

Format per entry:
```
### YYYY-MM-DD
- Fundamentals: mcap, ROCE/ROE, growth, debt (whatever's material/new)
- Technicals: weekly/monthly MACD + Bollinger Bands read, or RSI/CCI/Williams %R proxy
- Red flag tier: none / HIGH CAUTION / AVOID / EXCLUDE — one line why, if any
- Community signal: forum sentiment + any high-conviction call from a top contributor (see top-contributors.md)
- Thesis fit: 10x-in-2-3-years / 100x-in-10-years / neither, one line why
- Source: [thread title](url)
```

Stocks are ordered alphabetically by slug.

- **Dynamic Cables** (legacy Tier A) - power cables (HV 55% / LV 37%) + conductors on the transmission + solar capex super-cycle. ~30% revenue growth confirmed across FY25-26 concalls, Q1FY27 rev +33% / PAT +37%, ROCE ~27%, long-term debt fully repaid, RM cost passed through in real time both ways (answers the thin-GPM sector concern). UL 854 (US) + PGCIL conductor approvals validate recent capex; capacity debottlenecked toward ~Rs 1,600 Cr, 6x-asset-turn greenfield H2 FY27. Offsets: ~Rs 344 Cr corporate guarantee to Indokrates Pvt Ltd (promoter's 53-yr-old related company - forum researched, not deemed fraudulent, but sizeable) and repeated capex-commissioning slippage. 3 elevated-tier users (kartik_bhat, Jitendrapd94, Tushar_Gupta1) posted consistent constructive concall coverage; 2 trusted_signals logged (elevated, no floor). thesis_fit **10x-in-2-3-years** (multi-year framing), conviction **Medium-High**. revisit_after_30d=true. No red flag.
- **Harshdeep Hortico** - Rs ~130 Cr BSE-SME garden-decor micro-cap (pots/planters/shade nets/outdoor furniture + new Agri Shield, Muebles). ~25% ROE, ~24% ROIC, near debt-free, H1FY26 rev +31% / PAT +35%, combination moat (2,200+ SKU mould library + nationwide distribution + institutional airport/hotel/builder relationships). Negative FCF - working capital / inventory absorbing operating cash during scale-up. abhicnr (elevated) authored the founding thesis (7 loves) and defended it; logged as trusted_signal (elevated, no floor). thesis_fit **10x-in-2-3-years**, conviction **Medium**, unconfirmed (SME scale + negative FCF). No red flag.
- **Exicom Tele-Systems** - loss-making EV-charger (incl. Australian Tritium) + telecom Critical Power. FY25 ROCE 7.3% / ROE 3.3%, CARE downgrade to BBB/A3 Negative on debt-funded Tritium buy. But Q4 FY26 = first-ever consolidated EBITDA breakeven, standalone EBITDA +148%, Tritium +157% QoQ, ~Rs 1,000 Cr order book, FY28 data-centre pipeline. RocketMan (elevated) posted (7 loves) an AI-datacentre rectifier-adjacency thesis - "no position, recently started tracking". thesis_fit **10x-in-2-3-years**, conviction **Low**, unconfirmed (recent-inflection rule). No red-flag tier (CARE downgrade is a caution).
- **Cosmic CRF** - BSE-SME CRF / railway-wagon-component maker rolling up toward "integrated wagon manufacturer" (NS Engineering, Cosmic Springs, forging, contested Amzen NCLT bid). FY25 sales +58% / PAT +142% off a tiny base, FY25 guidance missed ~20%, thin 6-7% PAT margin, negative FY25 OCF/FCF (funding subsidiaries via advances). **HIGH CAUTION**: NCLAT Section 29A(a)(c)(h)(j) ineligibility finding for Amzen (now sub judice in Supreme Court) + public forgery/unauthorised-share-transfer allegations by the Raft Motors founder against the Cosmic-linked Raft Cosmic EV JV + recurring delayed disclosures. Manik_Gupta (elevated) in-thread only with legal-status/risk posts. thesis_fit **10x-in-2-3-years** on asymmetric-optionality basis only, conviction **Low**.
- **Praveg** - asset-heavy tent-resort operator (~75% of resorts on short govt land leases), slid into losses (ROE -2.4%), still ~100-111x earnings vs Indian Hotels ~60x, no moat / no hospitality pedigree. **HIGH CAUTION**: depreciation-policy change (extended asset lives) that inflates PAT, 6-7 warrant/preferential dilution rounds in 2023-24 despite being debt-free, promoter-entity merger lifting promoter stake >50%, 2023 KMP + internal-auditor resignations, ex-MD Rs 100 Cr defamation suit vs promoter. thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Low**.
- **Sanghvi Movers** - largest India crane-rental co (~450 cranes); best-ever FY26 (rev Rs 1,020 Cr +37%, EBITDA Rs 429 Cr), KSA ops began Q2 FY26 at ~100% utilisation (Jeddah Tower, ~$800m-1bn/yr market), core rental ~50-56% EBITDA, ~15x P/E, clean governance (no pledge/sale, next-gen mgmt). Capital-heavy (FY26-27 capex ~Rs 1,000 Cr, D/E up to 0.46, interest +73% YoY), historically loss-making when wind collapsed, ~15% ROE. Real KSA catalyst but a 10x needs ~Rs 38,000 Cr. thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Medium**.
- **Syrma SGS** - diversified EMS executing a genuine margin turnaround (EBITDA margin ~6% -> 9-11%+ via deliberate mix-shift out of low-margin consumer into auto/healthcare/industrial + ODM at 16%); FY26 EBITDA guided >Rs 500 Cr (+55-57% vs 30% target), Q3 FY26 EBITDA +101%, exports +66%; MSI/Dynabook laptop assembly, Rs 1,595 Cr Andhra PCB fab, Rs 1,000 Cr QIP, PCB anti-dumping duty. But ~Rs 28,400 Cr mcap, ~10-12% ROE, ~4-5% PAT margin, stock already doubled in 6 months. Elevated users (DeepVal, dpk1994, Krishna_Kumar) track positively; 1 trusted_signal logged (elevated, no floor). thesis_fit **neither** (size/returns), conviction **Medium**.
- **Waaree Renewable Technologies** (legacy excluded_large) - Waaree group solar-EPC (+ BESS, T&D). FY26 revenue ~2x FY25, ROE ~50%, near debt-free - but a justified multi-year de-rating from ~250x to sub-25x P/E as the market re-prices it to EPC multiples; EBITDA guided down ~19% -> 15%, solar-EPC order book shrinking YoY (ALMM-II front-loading + grid-evacuation bottleneck), management evasive on new orders, CFO (2025) + Data-Centre CEO (2026) departures, defensible-but-profit-flattering GST capitalisation. karanshah137 (elevated) "never invested due to high P/E". thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Medium**.
- **Avalon Technologies** - dual-shore (US Atlanta + India) high-mix EMS; 8 straight sequential-growth quarters, Q1 FY27 rev +50% YoY, FY27 guide raised to 26-30%, box-build 60% of revenue, ROCE ~20-23%, EN 15085-2 rail cert. kdjolly (elevated) authored strong thread notes (12 loves) but no sized/conviction call. ~Rs 14,900 Cr mcap + premium multiple => 10x needs ~Rs 1.5 lakh crore. thesis_fit **neither** (size), conviction **Medium**.
- **Elantas Beck India** - ALTANA-group MNC in electrical-insulation resins/wire enamels (ABB/Siemens/Bosch/Enercon); ROCE ~21%+, near debt-free, ~19% 5-yr PAT CAGR, Q1 FY27 rev +35%. ~Rs 9,300 Cr at ~50x earnings - quality fully priced; thread dormant since 2020. thesis_fit **neither** (size/valuation), conviction **Medium**.
- **Bharat Bijlee** - transformers + motors + KEB-partnered drives + a ~Rs 1,780 Cr listed-equity book. Reported ROE ~7% but ex-investments ROIC ~27% (forum estimate) - a cheap operating business + asset play + holdco discount; transformer up-cycle + capacity 18k -> 28k MVA. Against: employee costs ~2.5x peers (Voltamp), copper/CRGO margin squeeze now, no concall / minimal disclosure, slow to deploy ~Rs 2,000 Cr surplus. thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Medium**.
- **Savita Oil Technologies** - debt-free specialty petroleum (transformer oils / white oils / Savsol lubricants); ROE ~10%, ~2% recent growth, multiple buybacks. Transformer-oil / biodegradable-ester / plastic-recycling optionality is entirely prospective, not in the numbers. No forum activity since Aug 2024 - prior thread read stands. thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Medium**.
- **Thangamayil Jewellery** - Madurai-anchored TN gold retailer (~66 stores); strong FY25-26 (rev +28-45%, swung back to profit, record Oct-2025 sales ~Rs 1,032 Cr), candid capital-disciplined family mgmt, Rs 510 Cr rights issue improved gearing. But ~6% EBITDA margin (structural), topline growth partly gold-price-driven, and Kalyan/Titan/Joyalukkas all expanding into TN. Reported mcap looks rich vs EBITDA (flagged for verification). thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Medium**.
- **United Foodbrands (Barbeque Nation)** - loss-making dine-in buffet chain (230+ outlets), ~12 consecutive quarters of negative SSSG, EBITDA down 3 years, swung from cash to ~Rs 80 Cr net debt while opening 35 stores; ICRA outlook cut to Negative. Recent SSSG ~+28.7% is off a deeply depressed base; elevated users in-thread (StonePitbull "trapped", RocketMan "risk-reward unattractive") net-negative. thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Low**. No red-flag tier.
- **Priti International** - Rs ~45 Cr micro-cap wood/metal/textile furniture & handicraft exporter; debt-free but -7% 5-yr sales CAGR, ~7% ROE, declining promoter holding, rising receivables, no catalyst; thread dormant since 2024. thesis_fit **neither**, conviction **Low**.

### Tooling fix [2026-08-31] — generic threads no longer resurface every run
Root cause: `discover.py`'s actionable-report loop filtered only on forum-activity recency + `last_analyzed_date` cooldown, never on `status`. A `candidate` that is genuinely not a single company never gets a `last_analyzed_date`, and if its thread is busy (e.g. investing-basics, 1847 replies) it never leaves the 30-day window either → it was "actionable" on every run, indefinitely (11-12 such threads each run). Fixes: (1) new `status: "not-a-stock"` terminal disposition + `valuepickr-screen/scripts/mark_not_a_stock.py`; `discover.py` now skips `TERMINAL_SKIP_STATUSES` in both the scan and actionable loops. (2) Tightened `TITLE_BLACKLIST` + new anchored `TITLE_BLACKLIST_REGEX` for sector/cycle/screener-list/"India 2040" patterns (verified against live forum: catches the 9 real generic threads, frees Exicom / India Shelter Finance / TIL false positives). (3) New "STALE CANDIDATES" report section: never-analyzed candidates >45d old are quarantined out of the batch for one-time triage. (4) Marked the 12 current offenders `not-a-stock`. (5) SKILL.md Step 1.3 documents the disposal step. Post-fix `discover.py`: `terminal-status (skipped): 12`, `actionable now: 0`.

- New-unit ramp ahead of industry: Faridabad Sec-20 EBITDA breakeven ~9 months, Agra 20%+ margin first full quarter, Model Town breakeven guided H2 FY27. Bed target accelerated to 5,000+ within ~2.5 years.

- Unresolved: FY26 FCF -Rs 112 Cr (5th straight negative year); ~Rs 1,400 Cr bed capex vs that OCF — management claims internal-accrual funding / "no fresh loans", unproven.
- NEW: 28-Aug-2026 media reports Advent International + Aster DM in due diligence for a controlling stake (company denied being in sale talks); stock to a new all-time high ~Rs 1,000; a control sale triggers a 26% open offer. Deepens the promoter-commitment concern (after Nov-2025 discounted 5.85% sale).
- Reported EBITDA margin compressed 29% (FY24) -> ~24% (FY26) on new-hospital drag (adjusted ~28%).
- conviction Medium-High -> Medium-High (stronger fundamentals offset by binary M&A overlay at ~53x P/E); thesis_fit 10x-in-2-3-years -> neither.

## Vivid Electromech Ltd — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-02]
- Filings confirm the equity story: OPM stepped 9% -> 18% -> 23% across FY24-26 as COGS fell ~85% -> ~64% on a genuine mix shift to data-centre PDUs / DWDM / MV panels — structural, not a spike (a later quarter printed ~19%).
- Dated catalyst now in hand: Ambernath facility (3x verticals, 7,500 -> 22,500/yr) — Phase 1 by end-Aug 2026, full commissioning Oct 2026; FY27 revenue explicitly guided >Rs 300 Cr (~+50%).
- Q1 FY27 revenue Rs 32.7 Cr (+36% YoY) looks soft but the year is heavily back-ended; order intake in the quarter (STT Rs 30.1 Cr PDU/DWDM, Univastu Rs 20.2 Cr, Cosmic PV / solar / wastewater Rs 52.8 Cr) ran ahead of the flat Rs 210 Cr executable book.
- New caveat: working-capital-heavy — ~212 debtor days and gross debt up to ~Rs 33 Cr (from ~Rs 4 Cr); the "almost debt-free" framing was too clean. Short listed history (Apr 2026), no guidance track record.
- Valuation ~44x trailing / ~25-30x FY27E; phreakv6 (core-tier) buying under Rs 1,500, floor active.
- conviction Medium-High -> Medium-High (confirmatory deep read; not raised to High on SME liquidity + full multiple + debtor days).

## Fredun Pharmaceuticals — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-03]
- FY26 inflection real at annual level: revenue +40.1% to Rs 639 Cr, PAT +60%, EBITDA margin +276bps to 14.83% on structural mix shift to new-age verticals.
- BUT Q1 FY27 EBITDA margin 14.36%, only +18bps YoY — the margin-doubling leg happened FY25->FY26 and has plateaued; management flagged one-time advance orders at favourable pricing in the +90% Q1 revenue.

- conviction Medium-High -> Medium; thesis_fit 10x-in-2-3-years retained.

## Jeena Sikho Lifecare — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-03]
- FY26 filings substantiate the community's cash-flow-quality case: OCF Rs 254 Cr > PAT Rs 222 Cr, FCF Rs 221 Cr on ~Rs 34 Cr capex; net-cash balance sheet (net debt -Rs 108 Cr); maiden dividend declared.
- Zero promoter encumbrance formally confirmed for FY26 — directly rebuts the thread's "seems shady" framing. No auditor issue; CS resignation Jul-2026 was a routine KMP change.
- Growth has clearly decelerated: FY26 consolidated +71% → Q1 FY27 +29%; core Healthcare Services segment only +13% to Rs 106 Cr despite private-pay IPD admissions +33%. Government-panel Panchakarma cut deliberately.
- Products/e-commerce (~86% GM) is now ~53% of revenue and the faster-growing leg — mix, not hospitals, drove the Q1 print. EBITDA margin normalising (FY26 44% → Q1 FY27 ~41%); FY26 margins look like a peak.
- Market has de-rated the stock ~34% over the trailing year to ~26x — a standing skepticism discount.
- conviction Medium -> Medium; thesis 10x-in-2-3-years kept but now caveated on Healthcare Services re-accelerating. mcap tier → Mid-cap (~Rs 6,235 Cr).

## JNK India Ltd — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-03]
- Capital efficiency reappraised sharply down: ROCE ~17.5% / ROE ~12.1% (FY26), vs the ~53%/~45% in the prior write-up — that was a FY22-23 pre-scale figure. ROCE trend: 54% (FY22) → 15-18% (FY24-26).
- Operating cash flow negative or breakeven three years running: FY24 -Rs 10 Cr, FY25 -Rs 65 Cr, FY26 -Rs 2 Cr. Working-capital-heavy EPC-style model; debtor days did improve 261 → 139.
- Q1 FY27 consolidated revenue Rs 186 Cr (+80.6%) but off a soft base; EBITDA margin 11.8% (vs 7%), PAT Rs 9.6 Cr. JNK Chemdist JV: 8.8% of revenue but Rs 3.6 Cr operating loss, breakeven only targeted by FY27-end.
- FY27 guidance reaffirmed at 20-25% revenue growth / 12-14% EBITDA margin — below the 25-60% range the prior note cited and below trusted user Satishwe's 30-35%.
- Order book Rs 1,801 Cr (~2.2x sales) is real; Dangote Phase 2 still only "under discussion", not won. RPT dependence on promoter JNK Global rising, cooperation agreement un-renewed, JNK Global has board disputes.
- ~32x trailing P/E already prices in unwon catalysts. conviction Medium-High -> Medium; thesis 10x-in-2-3-years -> neither (base case 2-4x). mcap tier Small-cap (~Rs 2,416 Cr). No red flag.

## KMC Speciality Hospitals — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-03]
- Board approved (14-Aug-2026) a Rs 519 Cr / ~591-bed expansion: Rs 486 Cr / ~500 beds Trichy (Rs 422 Cr Super-Speciality Block + Rs 64 Cr Mother & Child) + Rs 33 Cr / 91-bed Bengaluru Mother & Child hospital. Commissioning phased Q2 FY28 → Q2 FY30; funded via internal accruals + term debt. Directly answers the long-standing "single asset / finite runway" bear thesis.
- Q1 FY27: revenue Rs 91.8 Cr (+37.9%), PAT Rs 16.57 Cr (+120% off soft base), PBT +124%; fourth straight quarter of 33-38% growth. Existing multi-specialty unit at ~98% occupancy; ARPOB +17% YoY to ~Rs 31,481.
- FY26: revenue Rs 306 Cr (+32%), EBITDA margin ~30% (from 25.6%), PAT Rs 47 Cr (+118%). ROCE ~20.3% (below 5-yr avg 24.4%), ROE ~25%. Negative working capital, OCF Rs 79 Cr > PAT. Net-debt/EBITDA ~1.25x. IND A+ / outlook Positive.
- Parent corporate guarantee stable at Rs 98.57 Cr (SBI loan) — governance concern contained, not worsening. Promoter 75%. Zero dividend.
- Held Medium-High (not raised to High): capex ~2.5x net worth, term-debt-funded, payoff FY28-FY30, Bengaluru an unproven competitive metro, stock at ~40x P/E / ~10.7x book, near-term growth capacity-capped. thesis 10x-in-2-3-years kept. mcap tier Small/Mid-cap (~Rs 2,251 Cr). No red flag.

- A fresh Rs1,000cr capital-raise resolution was approved 10-Aug-2026 (on top of an unfinished Nov-2024 QIP) — an incremental dilution risk not previously flagged.
- four_box (first time): 2.5 (tailwind/moat yes, tam/valuation weak/no) — thesis_fit kept at 10x-in-2-3-years only via the growth-un-ignorable exception (real, reported, accelerating financials + an already-realized FDA approval), capping conviction at Medium. Conviction Medium -> Medium (catalyst fully confirmed, but valuation ran up in lockstep so risk-adjusted picture is unchanged).

## Vinati Organics Ltd — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-05]
- Real quality improvement since the initial screen: ROCE 18.14%->21.4%, ROE 14.51%->16.2%, and valuation actually de-rated from the community-flagged 42x to ~27-31x trailing P/E — a meaningfully cheaper entry than before.

- Added four_box (first time): 3.5 (tailwind/moat/valuation yes, tam weak) — technically clears the >=3.0 bar, but thesis_fit kept at "neither" by explicit analyst override (same logic as Nesco): 15% guided volume growth and 3% 3-yr CAGR don't support a 10x/100x return magnitude. Conviction Medium-High -> Medium-High (real positives and a real margin miss roughly offset). Flagged a likely data cross-contamination in trusted_signals ("Goregaon land" reference doesn't match this company) for the owning task to check.

## ADF Foods Ltd — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-05]
- FY26/TTM data reveal genuine operating leverage the initial screen's "steady compounder" framing missed: FY26 PAT +29.84% YoY on revenue +15.88%; TTM profit growth (38%) is running ~2x TTM sales growth (19%), both well ahead of their 5-yr averages.
- Surat Greenfield frozen-foods plant is commissioned (Mar-2026, +10,000MT, total 38,000MT) and ramping — up to Rs250cr incremental revenue potential (~35% of FY26 base) over a 2-3 year ramp, only ~30% utilization expected in FY27.
- US subsidiary received a USD2.1mn (~Rs20cr) tariff refund (Jul-2026). Q1 FY27 was indeed muted by Middle East conflict/container shortages (revenue still +25.9% YoY) as previously flagged, but FY27 guide of >Rs900cr (~32% growth) is explicitly conditional on stabilization. FCF has actually been negative 2 years running (FY26 -Rs25cr, FY25 -Rs4cr) funding the plant — a real nuance vs the "debt-free, cash-rich" framing (there is Rs55cr of modest borrowings, still net-cash).
- Valuation essentially unchanged (~30x trailing P/E) despite the acceleration — market hasn't re-rated yet; reverse-P/E needs only ~9.5% CAGR vs actual 30-38% delivered, gap_direction underestimated.
- Added four_box (first time): 4.0, a clean pass on all boxes — but thesis_fit kept "neither" by analyst override (same logic as Vinati/Nesco): this is linear, capacity/distribution-driven growth, not the asymmetric-optionality kind the 10x label is meant for. Conviction raised Medium -> Medium-High on the real, evidenced acceleration.

## Bhagyanagar India Ltd — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-05]
- Management's own Q1 FY27 concall guidance — not just a forum counter-argument — frames the standout ~Rs72,000/tonne EBITDA quarter as one-off, normalizing toward a ~5% EBITDA margin, consistent with FY26's actual ~4.5% full-year print; directly corroborates rather than merely being countered by the bear case.
- New finding: FY25 operating cash flow was negative (~-Rs143cr) despite reported profit growth, with a ~Rs179cr working-capital drain — a genuine earnings-quality gap not previously captured; debt/equity ~1.14x with rising interest costs.
- New finding: the entire copper business is mid-process of a 1:1 demerger into a new, separately-listed entity, Tieramet Ltd (NCLT approved Jan-2026, shareholders approved Mar-2026, completion/listing timeline still undisclosed) — the current ticker will retain only wind-power assets and land parcels once complete.
- ROCE/ROE data conflicts sharply across sources this pass (6.32%/~12% 3yr-avg vs the 20.7%/21.6% recorded at initial screen) — left quality_metrics.roce_pct null pending reconciliation to a primary filing rather than guess.
- four_box score unchanged at 1.5 (weak tailwind, weak TAM, no moat, weak valuation) — hard-caps thesis_fit at "neither" per the quick-ref decision table, correcting a prior inconsistency where thesis_fit had been recorded as 10x-in-2-3-years despite the same score.
- conviction Medium -> Low-Medium.

- conviction Medium -> Medium (held).

## Alufluoride Ltd — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-05]
- FY26 was a record year (standalone revenue Rs208.76Cr +10.7%, PAT Rs24.29Cr +32.7%, ROCE 24-25% for 3 years), and the 24,000 TPA capacity expansion completed Aug 2026.
- But Q1 FY27 revenue fell ~40% YoY (Rs25.97Cr vs Rs42.93Cr) on reduced production - root cause: primary contracted FSA feedstock supplier Coromandel International has been unable to supply its full contracted 4,000 TPA quantity "since inception," forcing costlier/uncertain balance sourcing from Odisha. This is a structural supply-reliability problem, not the raw-material-price-volatility framing the Jan-2026 trusted-user writeup and the planned Alumina Hydrate backward-integration project were built around.
- The new 24,000 TPA capacity may not be reliably fed if this persists - a real execution risk on top of the capex already spent (FY26 capex ~Rs59Cr against OCF of only Rs15Cr, cash conversion weakened ~125%->~59%).
- four_box moat downgraded yes->weak, score 3.5->3.0 (thesis_fit 10x-in-2-3-years retained, score still >=3.0). conviction cut Medium -> Low-Medium pending confirmation the feedstock shortfall actually resolves.

## Harshdeep Hortico Ltd — deep-dive pass 1 [2026-09-07]
- FY26: revenue Rs 68.7 Cr (+22% YoY), PAT Rs 12.5 Cr (+29%), PBT +41%, EBITDA Rs 19.8 Cr (~29% margin, +32%), net worth Rs 59.9 Cr (+44%), ROE 23.3%.
- Cash-flow concern now evidenced: FY26 operating cash flow was NEGATIVE Rs 8.4 Cr *before* capex of Rs 8.5 Cr; company drew ~Rs 3.8 Cr new secured loans to bridge. FCF deeply negative.
- Growth decelerated through the year: H1 revenue ~+31-35% / PAT +35% -> H2 revenue +15% / PAT +25%.
- Capacity expanded: total moulding 1,440 t/yr, shade-net line 3.5 lakh kg/yr, Agri Shield +200 t; new categories — India-first rotomoulded decorative fountains (Apr 2026), Muebles furniture. Airport contracts (Mumbai, Mangalore). Second dividend (Rs 0.25/sh).
- Valuation genuinely cheap: ~10x FY26 P/E, ~6.5x EV/EBITDA, ~2.2x P/B — prices in near-zero growth. four_box 3.5 (moat downgraded to weak; valuation upgraded to yes).
- conviction Medium -> Medium (cheap multiple + real growth offset by evidenced negative CFO and H2 deceleration).

Tiers 1-4 and the re-verification backlog empty. Theme-rank: only galaxy-bearings hit a keyword ("bearing" -> Humanoids/robotics-bearings P1) but the match is nominal (commodity CV/agri bearings, not precision/robotics content) — the rest theme_rank 2, taken in recency order. Cross-check: none of the 10 appear in legacy-108-screen final-ranking tiers/avoid.

Outcome: **all 10 -> thesis_fit "neither"** this run. Notable individual reads:

**shaily-engineering** (tier 4.55, @unseenvalue high) — genuinely excellent business: GLP-1 pen-injector picks-and-shovels, 23-24 pharma device partners for the 2026+ semaglutide patent cliff, trailing ROCE ~29% (spot 42%), ROE ~27-33%, revenue +27-34% for several quarters, EBITDA margin +~1,000bps YoY. UBS Buy Rs 4,000, Capital Group buying. But ~Rs 14,000 Cr mid-cap trading rich near its 52-week high, a real oral-GLP-1 (Wegovy pill) cannibalisation debate on the thread, promoter selling, and Q3FY26 the first QoQ wobble. four_box 3.5 (valuation weak) — score permits a return label, judgment declines it on size/valuation math. thesis_fit "neither", conviction Medium. value_chain block added (GLP-1 device layer). Better suited to a quality-compounder bucket than the max-returns screen.

**pitti-engineering** (tier 4.55, @suru27 high) — organised electrical-steel lamination market leader (~8% share, target 10-12%) executing a deliberate value-chain-up shift to 15%+ stator/rotor assemblies; ~24% revenue CAGR, ~19% ROCE/ROE, post-merger (Pitti Castings + Bagadia Chaitra + Dakshin Foundry) integration, railway/renewable/power tailwinds, deleveraging. VP thread stale (last post Apr-2026). But management itself states no pricing power (fixed EBITDA/tonne, RM pass-through), the organised TAM (~500k tonne) is finite, and promoters are selling ~Rs 1,400+. four_box 3.0 — label declined on unit economics. thesis_fit "neither", conviction Medium. value_chain block added.

**chatha-foods** (discovery, no theme match — QSR-consumption proxy) — small-cap (~Rs 250 Cr) B2B frozen-food supplier to Jubilant/Domino's, Subway, Burger King; ~20% QSR-industry proxy, a 3x capacity expansion (to ~16,000 MTPA) just physically completed = large latent operating leverage for FY27-28, unusually transparent/honest promoter (publishes closed analyst-call transcripts), Marathondreams (elevated tier) accumulating for ~1 year. But the operating leverage is anticipated, not yet demonstrated in the numbers; the new veg capacity has no disclosed committed clients; a surprise B2C/D2C "Unifayre" pivot contradicts prior B2B-only discipline and receivables have doubled YoY. Management's own guidance implies ~3x, not 10x. four_box 3.0 — return-magnitude label deferred pending FY27 numbers per the trailing-avg/live-inflection rule (catalyst in place, inflection not shown). thesis_fit "neither", conviction Medium. value_chain block added. trusted_signal logged for Marathondreams (sustained accumulate, not a core-tier 10x call).

**pc-jewellers** (discovery) — **HIGH CAUTION red flag.** Operational turnaround is visible (QoQ improvement 3-4 quarters, debt cleared, showrooms reopening) but the governance record is egregious: alleged ~Rs 1,500 Cr prior-promoter siphoning, historical SEBI insider-trading action, and an auditor emphasis of matter (Q3FY25) on ~Rs 1,514 Cr of export proceeds delayed beyond FEMA timelines (since regularised via condonation + ~Rs 7 Cr penalty). Debt was eliminated by equity dilution, not cash flow; multi-year revenue is declining; experienced VP members (The_Seeker, MHS, Cshar) explicitly call it a fraud/value trap. four_box 0.5 -> thesis_fit "neither" hard, conviction Low, red_flag_tier "HIGH CAUTION".

**galaxy-bearings** (discovery, nominal theme keyword hit) — tiny export-oriented commodity auto/agri bearing maker; ~1.5% revenue growth over 5 years, ~7.7% ROCE, ~206 debtor days. The 4.5x capacity expansion (2.1M -> 9.6M bearings/yr) has been stuck in CWIP for 2+ years with the FY25 auditor flagging it as a Key Audit Matter; Nov-2024 US OFAC sanctions (since lifted) showed forex fragility for a 75%+ exporter; a governance question is open on an undisclosed CEO designate. four_box 2.0 -> "neither" hard, conviction Low. No value_chain block (nominal keyword match only).

**asian-oilfields / Asian Energy Services** (discovery) — turnaround upstream energy-services (seismic -> O&M / integrated field development / coal-handling infra); order book ~8x revenue, FY26 revenue +70% — but acquisition-led (Kuiper), standalone roughly flat, and management missed its own "exceptional growth" guide. The pending Oilmax merger dilutes equity (promoter to ~47%) on subjective valuation of fields not yet in production. Near the 52-week high at ~32-38x. four_box 1.5 -> "neither" hard, conviction Low-Medium.

**andhra-sugar** (discovery, no theme match) — well-managed, near-debt-free integrated chlor-alkali chemicals company (sugar <10% of revenue) at a cyclical earnings trough (3-yr ROE ~4.5%). Mild catalysts: a possible caustic-soda cycle turn (latest quarter +13%) and an aspirin US-business restart by end-2026 (~900 MT/annum). No structural growth engine, minimal disclosure (no concalls), and the stock is already up ~50% off its low. four_box 2.0 -> "neither", conviction Low-Medium. A value/dividend hold, not a screen candidate.

**inox-wind** (discovery, no theme match — wind equipment is not an active_themes.json entry) — wind-turbine OEM turned profitable after the 2016-22 downturn; 3.3-3.5 GW order book, broadened IPP base, deleveraged, CRISIL outlook "Positive". But ~5.7% ROE on a ~31x P/E, a long record of destroying long-term shareholder value (the thread's own holders are asking for "red flags"), a management strategy flip-flop (anti-solar then into solar), the ISTS-charge waiver ending, and a post-Gensol sector de-rating; down ~49% over a year. four_box 2.0 -> "neither", conviction Low-Medium. karanshah137 (elevated) Q3FY26 post logged as informational, not a call.

**jindal-saw** (tier 4.5 external-lead, untrusted @nareshbahrain retrospective "I called it" post) — large-cap SAW/DI/seamless pipe maker, statistically cheap (~Rs 1,500 Cr PAT, low-teens P/E, ~Rs 11,400 Cr reserves, low LT debt, ~Rs 13,500 Cr order book). But management acknowledges the top line has plateaued, expansions are 2-3 years out, near-term water-scheme spending has slowed, and it has visibly lagged peer Welspun Corp on execution; a DI-pipe CCI antitrust probe was referenced. Large-cap default-to-neither applies (no specific named management/structural inflection — the GCC/brownfield capex is ordinary). four_box 1.5 -> "neither" hard, conviction Low-Medium.

**beezaasan-explotech** (tier 4.5 external-lead, untrusted @Rahul_Invest, no VP thread — web-only pass) — newly-listed high-return commercial-explosives microcap (ROCE ~23%, ROE ~21%, D/E 0.24; cement/mining/defence/BRO/PSU), the profile of an early-stage compounder in the same licensed, structurally-growing industry that produced Solar Industries. But the lead is an untrusted X tip with a multibagger framing, the stock has already run ~85% to an all-time high at ~40x earnings, it is newly listed with limited disclosure, and no primary documents (RHP/annual report/concalls) could be read this pass. four_box 2.0 — un-ignorable-growth exception could be argued but declined without primary-doc verification. thesis_fit "neither", conviction Low-Medium — **explicitly flagged for promotion to a full primary-doc deep-dive** (not a dismissal).
